# Content trace: Germany and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.2** It holds a role in all ten strategic compacts and a core seat in seven, and 93 economies count it as their best-placed trusted partner somewhere in the chain.
